- **forecaster**: 投研助手的概率判断员。只读一份冻结的问题契约和证据包，给出一个 P(YES)。不检索、不看研究过程叙述。仅用于没有可成交价格的假设，由 research 技能在概率判断步骤每条假设独立调用 3 次。 ([drewliu123/whymoved](https://github.com/drewliu123/whymoved/blob/HEAD/agents/forecaster.md) · ★1)

Расчёт выполняет MCP-сервер nabu-analytics (Holt linear trend, TypeScript, локально) — LLM только интерпретирует. Не гарантирует будущее, честно о неопределённости. Приватные ряды — только локально. - **foreshadowing-lifecycle-audit**: This audit checks NovelMemory for historical foreshadowing lifecycle pollution: the same foreshadowing id being planted or registered in more than one chapter. - **foresight**: - Path Resolution - Cognitive Mission - Role in the System - Handoff Protocol - Operating Rules - HITL Escalation Triggers - Memory Query - Output Contract - Predictions for Calibration (3.0) - Quality Metrics - Failure Modes to Avoid - Operational Parameters ([fabiolalli/aits-agents](https://github.com/fabiolalli/aits-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/aits/agents/foresight.md) · ★14)
- **foresight**: Foresight Agent (Magenta) of the AITS system. Activate when multiple options exist and the future is uncertain — produces the options-scenarios matrix that ranks options by robustness across plausible futures. Triggered automatically by inviolable rule #5 when Green generates ≥ 4 viable options. Distinct from Predictive — Predictive simulates futures for one option; Foresight compares many options across many futures. Arbitrates Green-Black conflicts when options are being killed prematurely. ([fabiolalli/aits-agents](https://github.com/fabiolalli/aits-agents/blob/HEAD/spec/agents/foresight.md) · ★14)
- **foresight-advisor**: Foresight review of a client or document: unseen risks, implicit deadlines, opportunities. ([nedjo90/juriste-fiduciaire-mustafa-ekrem](https://github.com/nedjo90/juriste-fiduciaire-mustafa-ekrem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/foresight-advisor.md))
- **foresight-architect**: Use to turn validated coverage gaps into ready-to-run regression proposals (in iterative-tdd's format) and to refine the priority/feature/serial reorg plan for hindsight. Writes proposals under .tdd/foresight/proposals/&lt;slug&gt;/ and reorg corrections to reorg/overrides.json; never writes into .tdd/regression/. Does not implement code or run the TDD loop — proposals are handed to /tdd for that. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/foresight-architect.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-auditor**: Use to validate the existing regression corpus and interpret coverage results. Runs the deterministic foresight.py audit + coverage, then reviews the findings against the actual code — confirming real gaps, catching the heuristic's false "uncovered"/"weak" matches, and explaining what each broken regression entry needs to become replayable and sound. Read-only on source; writes only its own notes. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/foresight-auditor.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-cartographer**: Use to build the static inventory of what a product does — its features, use cases, UI elements, routes/screens — by reading the code and docs and running read-only shell. The FIRST agent in a /foresight run; re-invoked after the visual pass to backfill source_refs. Writes inventory.json + inventory.md and detects the test run command. Does not edit code, drive the app, or judge coverage. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/agents/foresight-cartographer.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-mobile-explorer**: Use to explore a running ANDROID or iOS app — walk its screens, capture the UI hierarchy and a screenshot per step, and map elements to use cases. Runs in Phase 2 of /foresight when a mobile target is given. Drives the app via documented external CLIs (Maestro/Appium, adb for Android, xcrun simctl for iOS). Enriches inventory.json and writes per-run screenshots + ui_map.json. Read-only by default. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/agents/foresight-mobile-explorer.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-visual-inspector**: Use after an exploration run to look at its screenshots the way a person would — enumerate every visually distinct UI element, write a user story for each, and merge them into inventory.json with screenshot provenance. Runs in Phase 2 of /foresight after the explorers; does not drive the app and does not edit source. Flags high-value stories as regression candidates. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/foresight-visual-inspector.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-web-explorer**: Explore a running WEB app through the Playwright MCP server — navigate its routes, exercise primary end-to-end flows, save a screenshot per step, and record what each UI element does. Runs in the foresight workflow when a web target URL is given. Enriches inventory.json with observed UI behavior and writes a per-run ui_map.json + screenshots. Read-only by default; never performs destructive actions on a real target. ([sobryan/foresight](https://github.com/sobryan/foresight/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/foresight-web-explorer.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **foresight-web-explorer**: Use to explore a running WEB app — navigate its routes, exercise primary end-to-end flows, screenshot every step, and record what each UI element does. - **forest-checker**: Reviews a built ForestOverlay task (or several from one branch) as a fresh context that did not write it - reads the task's contract and diff (tasks.py brief), re-runs the suites the diff touches, finds faults, scores the rubric and records accept / revise / block with tasks.py review. Use for every behaviour change (plugin, site, bot) once it is built, before it is pushed or released; give it only "Check T-n", or "Check T-a T-b ..." for the tasks of one branch (one diff, one verdict each).
